FROM (
    SELECT DISTINCT `u`.`town`
    FROM (
        SELECT `b`.`town`
        FROM `business_registers` AS `b`
        WHERE `b`.`town` <> ''
        UNION
        SELECT `s`.`town`
        FROM `shopper_registers` AS `s`
        WHERE `s`.`town` <> ''
    ) AS `u`
) AS `u0`

SELECT `b`.`business_profile_id` AS `businessprofile_id`, `b`.`bus_reg_id` AS `BusRegId`, `b0`.`business_name` AS `Businessname`, `b0`.`business_username` AS `Businessusername`, `b`.`business_location`, `b`.`business_about`, `b`.`banner_path`, `b`.`logo_path`, `b`.`profile_status`, `b`.`bus_cat_id` AS `BusCatId`, `b`.`bus_serv_id` AS `BusServId`, `b1`.`business_service_name` AS `Businessservice_name`, `b2`.`business_category_name` AS `Businesscategory_name`, `b0`.`currency` AS `Currency`
FROM `business_profiles` AS `b`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b0` ON `b`.`bus_reg_id` = `b0`.`bus_reg_id`
LEFT JOIN `business_services` AS `b1` ON `b`.`bus_serv_id` = `b1`.`bus_serv_id`
